SECTION 1: SHIPPING -->\n151:           <section id=\"shipping\" class=\"mb-5\" data-faq-section=\"shipping\" aria-labelledby=\"faqShippingHeading\">\n152:             <div class=\"ud-faq-section-header\">\n153:               <h2 id=\"faqShippingHeading\" class=\"h4 fw-bold text-uppercase mb-0\">01 // Shipping</h2>\n154:               <span class=\"ud-mono small\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">DISPATCH &amp; DELIVERY</span>\n155:             </div>\n156: \n157:             <div class=\"ud-faq-item is-open\">\n158:               <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"true\" aria-controls=\"faq-ship-1\">\n159:                 <span>How long does order processing and nationwide delivery take?</span>\n160:                 <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[-]</span>\n161:               </button>\n162:               <div id=\"faq-ship-1\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n163:                 <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n164:                   <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n165:                     All standard orders placed before 3:00 PM PKT are packed and dispatched the same business day. Delivery takes <strong>1â€“2 business days</strong> within Islamabad, Lahore, and Karachi, and <strong>3â€“5 business days</strong> for all other cities nationwide. During major seasonal drops, please allow an extra 24 hours for quality control and serial numbering.\n166:                   </div>\n167:                 </div>\n168:               </div>\n169:             </div>\n170: \n171:             <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n172:               <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ship-2\">\n173:                 <span>What are the shipping charges and free delivery threshold?</span>\n174:                 <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n175:               </button>\n176:               <div id=\"faq-ship-2\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n177:                 <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n178:                   <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n179:                     Standard tracked courier shipping is a flat rate of <strong>PKR 250</strong> across Pakistan. Orders above <strong>PKR 10,000</strong> automatically qualify for free express shipping. Cash on Delivery (COD) and prepaid card/wallet orders carry no hidden handling fees.\n180:                   </div>\n181:                 </div>\n182:               </div>\n183:             </div>\n184: \n185:             <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n186:               <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ship-3\">\n187:                 <span>Can I pick up an online drop order at a physical URBANDROPS store?</span>\n188:                 <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n189:               </button>\n190:               <div id=\"faq-ship-3\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n191:                 <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n192:                   <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n193:                     Yes. Same-day In-Store Pickup is available at our Islamabad (F-7 &amp; Centaurus), Lahore (Gulberg &amp; DHA Phase 5), and Karachi (Clifton &amp; DHA Phase 6) flagships. Bring your digital order confirmation and valid photo ID within 5 days of receiving the ready-for-pickup SMS.\n194:                   </div>\n195:                 </div>\n196:               </div>\n197:             </div>\n198:           </section>\n199: \n200:           <!-- SECTION 2: RETURNS -->\n201:           <section id=\"returns\" class=\"mb-5\" data-faq-section=\"returns\" aria-labelledby=\"faqReturnsHeading\">\n202:             <div class=\"ud-faq-section-header\">\n203:               <h2 id=\"faqReturnsHeading\" class=\"h4 fw-bold text-uppercase mb-0\">02 // Returns</h2>\n204:               <span class=\"ud-mono small\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">EXCHANGES &amp; REFUNDS</span>\n205:             </div>\n206: \n207:             <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n208:               <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ret-1\">\n209:                 <span>What is your 14-day return and size exchange policy?</span>\n210:                 <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n211:               </button>\n212:               <div id=\"faq-ret-1\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n213:                 <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n214:                   <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n215:                     You may request a return or size exchange within <strong>14 days</strong> of delivery. Items must be unworn, unwashed, free of cologne or marks, and returned with all original URBANDROPS hangtags, zip-locks, and dust bags intact. Size exchanges can also be completed immediately at any flagship store.\n216:                   </div>\n217:                 </div>\n218:               </div>\n219:             </div>\n220: \n221:             <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n222:               <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ret-2\">\n223:                 <span>Are limited-edition capsule drops eligible for refunds?</span>\n224:                 <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n225:               </button>\n226:               <div id=\"faq-ret-2\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n227:                 <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n228:                   <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n229:                     Numbered archive drops and collaboration capsules are eligible for <strong>size exchanges or store credit</strong> only (while replacement stock lasts), unless the garment arrived defective or damaged. Standard core collection pieces qualify for full refunds back to your original payment method or bank transfer for COD orders.\n230:                   </div>\n231:                 </div>\n232:               </div>\n233:             </div>\n234: \n235:             <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n236:               <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-ret-3\">\n237:                 <span>How quickly are refunds processed after inspection?</span>\n238:                 <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n239:               </button>\n240:               <div id=\"faq-ret-3\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n241:                 <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n242:                   <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n243:                     Once your return parcel arrives at our fulfillment hub, quality inspection takes up to <strong>48 hours</strong>. Approved refunds are issued within <strong>3â€“5 business days</strong> to your bank account, JazzCash/Easypaisa wallet, or original card, and you will receive an email confirmation receipt.\n244:                   </div>\n245:                 </div>\n246:               </div>\n247:             </div>\n248:           </section>\n249: \n250:           <!-- SECTION 3: SIZING -->\n251:           <section id=\"sizing\" class=\"mb-4\" data-faq-section=\"sizing\" aria-labelledby=\"faqSizingHeading\">\n252:             <div class=\"ud-faq-section-header\">\n253:               <h2 id=\"faqSizingHeading\" class=\"h4 fw-bold text-uppercase mb-0\">03 // Sizing</h2>\n254:               <span class=\"ud-mono small\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">FIT &amp; MEASUREMENTS</span>\n255:             </div>\n256: \n257:             <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n258:               <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-size-1\">\n259:                 <span>Do URBANDROPS hoodies and tees run true to size or oversized?</span>\n260:                 <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n261:               </button>\n262:               <div id=\"faq-size-1\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n263:                 <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n264:                   <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n265:                     Most URBANDROPS heavyweight tees (280 GSM) and French terry hoodies (480 GSM) are cut in a <strong>boxy, dropped-shoulder streetwear silhouette</strong>. Take your usual size for the intended relaxed drape, or size down one step if you prefer a closer standard fit.\n266:                   </div>\n267:                 </div>\n268:               </div>\n269:             </div>\n270: \n271:             <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n272:               <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-size-2\">\n273:                 <span>What are the exact chest and length measurements for tops?</span>\n274:                 <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n275:               </button>\n276:               <div id=\"faq-size-2\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n277:                 <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n278:                   <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n279:                     <p class=\"mb-2\">Reference flat-lay garment measurements (in inches, Â±0.5\" tolerance):</p>\n280:                     <div class=\"table-responsive\">\n281:                       <table class=\"table table-dark table-sm table-bordered border-secondary mb-0 ud-mono small\">\n282:                         <thead>\n283:                           <tr>\n284:                             <th>Size</th>\n285:                             <th>Chest Width</th>\n286:                             <th>Body Length</th>\n287:                             <th>Shoulder Drop</th>\n288:                           </tr>\n289:                         </thead>\n290:                         <tbody>\n291:                           <tr>\n292:                             <td>S</td>\n293:                             <td>22.5\"</td>\n294:                             <td>27.5\"</td>\n295:                             <td>21.5\"</td>\n296:                           </tr>\n297:                           <tr>\n298:                             <td>M</td>\n299:                             <td>24.0\"</td>\n300:                             <td>28.5\"</td>\n301:                             <td>22.5\"</td>\n302:                           </tr>\n303:                           <tr>\n304:                             <td>L</td>\n305:                             <td>25.5\"</td>\n306:                             <td>29.5\"</td>\n307:                             <td>23.5\"</td>\n308:                           </tr>\n309:                           <tr>\n310:                             <td>XL</td>\n311:                             <td>27.0\"</td>\n312:                             <td>30.5\"</td>\n313:                             <td>24.5\"</td>\n314:                           </tr>\n315:                         </tbody>\n316:                       </table>\n317:                     </div>\n318:                   </div>\n319:                 </div>\n320:               </div>\n321:             </div>\n322: \n323:             <div class=\"ud-faq-item\">\n324:               <button type=\"button\" class=\"ud-faq-trigger\" aria-expanded=\"false\" aria-controls=\"faq-size-3\">\n325:                 <span>Will heavyweight cotton pieces shrink after washing?</span>\n326:                 <span class=\"ud-faq-toggle-icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">[+]</span>\n327:               </button>\n328:               <div id=\"faq-size-3\" class=\"ud-faq-panel\">\n329:                 <div class=\"ud-faq-panel-inner\">\n330:                   <div class=\"ud-faq-answer\">\n331:                     All URBANDROPS 100% combed cotton jerseys and fleeces are <strong>enzyme pre-shrunk</strong> during production. To preserve garment dimensions and puff-print graphics, wash inside out on a cold gentle cycle (30Â°C max) and hang dry flatâ€”never tumble dry on high heat.\n332:                   </div>\n333:                 </div>\n334:               </div>\n335:             </div>\n336:           </section>\n337:         </div>\n338: \n339:         <!-- Sidebar Summary Card -->\n340:         <aside class=\"col-lg-4\">\n341:           <div class=\"ud-card p-4 mb-4\">\n342:             <span class=\"ud-eyebrow\">Quick Policy Summary</span>\n343:             <h3 class=\"h5 fw-bold mb-3\">Drop Standards</h3>\n344:             <ul class=\"list-unstyled d-flex flex-column gap-3 mb-4 small text-secondary\">\n345:               <li class=\"d-flex justify-content-between border-bottom border-secondary border-opacity-25 pb-2\">\n346:                 <span>Same-Day Dispatch</span>\n347:                 <span class=\"ud-mono text-white\">Before 3 PM PKT</span>\n348:               </li>\n349:               <li class=\"d-flex justify-content-between border-bottom border-secondary border-opacity-25 pb-2\">\n350:                 <span>Free Shipping</span>\n351:                 <span class=\"ud-mono text-white\">Orders &gt; PKR 10K</span>\n352:               </li>\n353:               <li class=\"d-flex justify-content-between border-bottom border-secondary border-opacity-25 pb-2\">\n354:                 <span>Return Window</span>\n355:                 <span class=\"ud-mono text-white\">14 Days Unworn</span>\n356:               </li>\n357:               <li class=\"d-flex justify-content-between\">\n358:                 <span>Fit Profile</span>\n359:                 <span class=\"ud-mono\" style=\"color: var(--ud-accent);\">Boxy Streetwear</span>\n360:               </li>\n361:             </ul>\n362:             <a href=\"contact-support.html\" class=\"btn ud-btn-cta w-100 mb-2\">Submit a Support Ticket</a>\n363:             <a href=\"store-locator.html\" class=\"btn ud-btn-outline w-100\">Find Nearest Flagship</a>\n364:           </div>\n365:         </aside>\n366:       </div>\n367:     </div>\n368:   </main>\n369: \n370:   <!-- Shared URBANDROPS Footer -->\n371:   <footer class=\"ud-footer\">\n372:     <div class=\"container\">\n373:       <div class=\"row g-4\">\n374:         <div class=\"col-lg-4\">\n375:           <a class=\"ud-brand mb-3\" href=\"../index.html\">\n376:             <img src=\"../images/Logo.png\" alt=\"URBANDROPS Logo\" class=\"ud-brand-logo\" onerror=\"this.classList.add('is-missing'); SHARED CART BADGE (localStorage 'cartCount')\n16:      ======================================================================== */\n17:   function getCartCount() {\n18:     try {\n19:       const raw = localStorage.getItem('cartCount');\n20:       const parsed = parseInt(raw, 10);\n21:       return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 0 ? parsed : 0;\n22:     } catch (err) {\n23:       return 0;\n24:     }\n25:   }\n26: \n27:   function renderCartBadge() {\n28:     const badges = document.querySelectorAll('[data-cart-badge]');\n29:     const count = getCartCount();\n30:     badges.forEach(function (badge) {\n31:       badge.textContent = String(count);\n32:       badge.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Cart items: ' + count);\n33:     });\n34:   }\n35: \n36:   function initCartBadge() {\n37:     renderCartBadge();\n38:     window.addEventListener('storage', function (event) {\n39:       if (event.key === 'cartCount') {\n40:         renderCartBadge();\n41:       }\n42:     });\n43:   }\n44: \n45:   /* ========================================================================\n46:      2. SHARED NAVBAR SEARCH BAR\n47:      ======================================================================== */\n48:   function showToast(message) {\n49:     const existing = document.getElementById('udSharedToast');\n50:     if (existing) existing.remove();\n51: \n52:     const toast = document.createElement('div');\n53:     toast.id = 'udSharedToast';\n54:     toast.className = 'ud-toast';\n55:     toast.setAttribute('role', 'status');\n56:     toast.textContent = message;\n57:     document.body.appendChild(toast);\n58: \n59:     window.setTimeout(function () {\n60:       if (toast.parentNode) toast.remove();\n61:     }, 3500);\n62:   }\n63: \n64:   function initNavbarSearch() {\n65:     const searchForm = document.getElementById('udNavSearchForm');\n66:     const searchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n67:     if (!searchForm || !searchInput) return;\n68: \n69:     searchForm.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {\n70:       event.preventDefault();\n71:       const query = searchInput.value.trim();\n72:       if (!query) {\n73:         searchInput.focus();\n74:         showToast('Enter a keyword to search URBANDROPS.');\n75:         return;\n76:       }\n77: \n78:       // If on FAQ page, sync with live FAQ search input\n79:       const faqSearchInput = document.getElementById('faqSearchInput');\n80:       if (faqSearchInput) {\n81:         faqSearchInput.value = query;\n82:         faqSearchInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));\n83:         faqSearchInput.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });\n84:         const visibleCount = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item')).filter(function (el) {\n85:           return el.style.display !== 'none';\n86:         }).length;\n87:         showToast(visibleCount + ' FAQ result(s) for \"' + query + '\".');\n88:         return;\n89:       }\n90: \n91:       // If on Store Locator page, match city or store name\n92:       const cityFilter = document.getElementById('cityFilter');\n93:       if (cityFilter) {\n94:         const lower = query.toLowerCase();\n95:         const cities = ['islamabad', 'lahore', 'karachi'];\n96:         const matchedCity = cities.find(function (c) {\n97:           return c.includes(lower) || lower.includes(c);\n98:         });\n99:         if (matchedCity) {\n100:           const properCity = matchedCity.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + matchedCity.slice(1);\n101:           cityFilter.value = properCity;\n102:           cityFilter.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));\n103:           showToast('Filtered stores for ' + properCity + '.');\n104:           return;\n105:         }\n106:       }\n107: \n108:       showToast('Searching catalog for \"' + query + '\"...');\n109:     });\n110:   }\n111: \n112:   /* ========================================================================\n113:      3. 'true' : 'false');\n400:       }\n401:       if (icon) {\n402:         icon.textContent = open ? '[-]' : '[+]';\n403:       }\n404:     }\n405: \n406:     triggers.forEach(function (trigger) {\n407:       trigger.addEventListener('click', function () {\n408:         const item = trigger.closest('.ud-faq-item');\n409:         if (!item) return;\n410:         const currentlyOpen = item.classList.contains('is-open');\n411:         setFaqState(item, !currentlyOpen);\n412:       });\n413:     });\n414: \n415:     // Live Keyword Search + Category filter buttons (All / Shipping / Returns / Sizing)\n416:     const categoryButtons = document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-filter]');\n417:     const sections = document.querySelectorAll('[data-faq-section]');\n418:     const allFaqItems = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item'));\n419:     const faqSearchInput = document.getElementById('faqSearchInput');\n420:     const faqSearchClear = document.getElementById('faqSearchClear');\n421:     const faqSearchCount = document.getElementById('faqSearchCount');\n422:     const resetFaqSearchBtn = document.getElementById('resetFaqSearchBtn');\n423:     const faqEmpty = document.getElementById('faqEmptyState');\n424: \n425:     let activeCategory = 'all';\n426: \n427:     function applyFaqFilters() {\n428:       const query = faqSearchInput ? faqSearchInput.value.trim().toLowerCase() : '';\n429:       let visibleCount = 0;\n430: \n431:       sections.forEach(function (section) {\n432:         const sectionKey = section.getAttribute('data-faq-section');\n433:         const categoryMatch = activeCategory === 'all' || activeCategory === sectionKey;\n434:         const sectionItems = Array.from(section.querySelectorAll('.ud-faq-item'));\n435:         let sectionVisibleCount = 0;\n436: \n437:         sectionItems.forEach(function (item) {\n438:           const keywordMatch = query === '' || item.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(query);\n439:           const shouldShow = categoryMatch && keywordMatch;\n440:           item.style.display = shouldShow ? '' : 'none';\n441:           if (shouldShow) {\n442:             sectionVisibleCount += 1;\n443:             visibleCount += 1;\n444:           }\n445:         });\n446: \n447:         section.style.display = sectionVisibleCount > 0 ? '' : 'none';\n448:       });\n449: \n450:       if (faqEmpty) {\n451:         faqEmpty.classList.toggle('d-none', visibleCount > 0);\n452:       }\n453: \n454:       if (faqSearchClear) {\n455:         faqSearchClear.classList.toggle('d-none', query.length === 0);\n456:       }\n457: \n458:       if (faqSearchCount) {\n459:         faqSearchCount.textContent =\n460:           'Showing ' + visibleCount + ' of ' + allFaqItems.length + ' ' + (allFaqItems.length === 1 ? 'Question' : 'Questions');\n461:       }\n462:     }\n463: \n464:     if (faqSearchInput) {\n465:       faqSearchInput.addEventListener('input', applyFaqFilters);\n466:     }\n467: \n468:     if (faqSearchClear) {\n469:       faqSearchClear.addEventListener('click', function () {\n470:         if (faqSearchInput) {\n471:           faqSearchInput.value = '';\n472:           faqSearchInput.focus();\n473:         }\n474:         const navSearchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n475:         if (navSearchInput) navSearchInput.value = '';\n476:         applyFaqFilters();\n477:       });\n478:     }\n479: \n480:     categoryButtons.forEach(function (btn) {\n481:       btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n482:         activeCategory = btn.getAttribute('data-faq-filter') || 'all';\n483: \n484:         categoryButtons.forEach(function (b) {\n485:           b.classList.toggle('active', b === btn);\n486:         });\n487: \n488:         applyFaqFilters();\n489:       });\n490:     });\n491: \n492:     if (resetFaqSearchBtn) {\n493:       resetFaqSearchBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n494:         activeCategory = 'all';\n495:         categoryButtons.forEach(function (b) {\n496:           b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-faq-filter') === 'all');\n497:         });\n498:         if (faqSearchInput) {\n499:           faqSearchInput.value = '';\n500:           faqSearchInput.focus();\n501:         }\n502:         const navSearchInput = document.getElementById('udNavSearchInput');\n503:         if (navSearchInput) navSearchInput.value = '';\n504:         applyFaqFilters();\n505:       });\n506:     }\n507: \n508:     applyFaqFilters();\n509:   }\n510: \n511:   /* ========================================================================\n512:      5. Switching error borders and text to target the predefined danger variable ensures strict adherence to required visual feedback specs.\\n\\nInitial implementation of `emailInput` triggers on input events, but extending this immediate feedback to `nameInput` and `messageInput` ensures uniform responsiveness across all fields. Shifting focus to combine `input` events with `blur` listeners balances live reactivity with a polished user experience.\\n\\nSubtle dark confirmation styling deviates from the explicit Volt Lime requirement. Applying solid accent coloring with dark typography guarantees a high-contrast streetwear aesthetic, matching active badges. Dynamically formatting the confirmation message with a randomized alphanumeric identifier completes the generation requirement.\\n\\nDefault light close buttons avoid invisibility risks against bright accent backgrounds. Directing attention to store locator requirements reveals missing interactive direction triggers and modal markup on store cards, requiring a dynamic Bootstrap modal implementation.\\n\\nPresence of the framework bundle ensures modal interactions run natively without extra initialization. Injecting direction triggers directly into store card markup unlocks seamless navigation routing, pairing dynamic data attributes with brand-aligned styling. Constructing a dedicated modal container enforces dark-surface aesthetics with lime accents, standardizing store detail presentation.\\n\\nNavbar search implementation only triggers on form submission rather than providing dynamic live filtering. Adding a dedicated search input directly within the FAQ section enables real-time accordion item filtering on input events. Incorporating dual Google Maps action links for both turn-by-turn directions and pin views ensures comprehensive navigation support on store cards.\\n\\nHarmonizing category filter pills with live text input enables simultaneous multi-criteria filtering across accordion content. {"step_index":23,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-28T04:08:33Z","model":"gemini-4-hydrogen-high","input_tokens":2829,"cache_read_tokens":87417,"output_tokens":7645,"thinking":"Line-by-line inspection of the template files, stylesheets, and scripts exposes multiple latent bugs, accessibility gaps, and responsiveness flaws. Initial assumption that the codebase was fully optimized is incorrect; several unused elements and missing image fallbacks require targeted remediation across the asset set.\n\nImage path verification shows the logo fallback depends heavily on inline error handlers. Adding defensive runtime checks guarantees zero console exceptions if asset resolution fails across pages.\n\nInline error handlers combined with asynchronous script listeners guarantee complete coverage for image resolution failures. Cart state retrieval requires defensive wrapper handling for restricted storage contexts, while search submission behavior needs expansion to cover domain-specific query terms across secondary pages.\n\nInitial assumption that location search handles partial neighborhood terms like neighborhoods correctly is flawed, since the current array matching strictly expects primary city names. Adding multi-level matching against store titles and addresses prevents dead-end searches. Furthermore, missing CDN dependencies could block modal interactions entirely, requiring a direct link fallback to guarantee functionality under offline constraints.\n\nCategory filter buttons lack `aria-pressed` attributes, leaving screen readers without state context during interactive filtering. Adding dynamic state toggles for these controls ensures proper accessibility compliance.\n\nSmooth scrolling alone leaves screen reader users stranded without focus transfer when the confirmation card renders. Adding programmatic focus management alongside live region updates bridges the accessibility gap for dynamic alerts. Meanwhile, cross-page navigation checks confirm relative paths align correctly with teammate-managed pages, though structural divergence in footer layouts requires standardization of copyright strings and disclosure notes across templates.\n\nReviewing footer structure reveals inconsistent spacing classes and unaligned grid offsets compared to the primary template. Standardizing the column classes ensures visual parity across secondary pages.\n\nStrict inspection of the footer markup shows the copyright string order deviates from the required specification. Adjusting the text sequence enforces compliance, while expanding the customer service links and adding the missing AI Image Disclosure note ensures complete alignment with secondary page standards.\n\nKeyboard accessibility audit exposes missing `:focus-visible` outlines across navbar links, buttons, FAQ triggers, map pins, and footer links. Relying solely on color shifts fails keyboard navigation requirements, demanding explicit, consistent focus rings for every interactive element.\n\nIntegrating a top-of-body skip link provides essential keyboard shortcut utility for primary content access. Collapsed accordion panels also require explicit hidden states to prevent screen readers from announcing dormant content.\n\nExplicitly tying the FAQ panels to their triggers via `aria-labelledby` and `role=\"region\"` guarantees proper screen reader context. Preserving normal tab flow across all interactive triggers, combined with optional arrow-key navigation, maintains intuitive keyboard accessibility. Heading hierarchy checks across secondary templates confirm semantic progression from main section titles to sidebar subheaders.\n\nCategory filter pills lack dynamic `aria-pressed` states for screen readers. Extending state toggling to store locator map pins and active focus triggers ensures clear spatial context for assistive technologies.\n\nContact form inputs feature proper labeling and `aria-describedby` associations, though linking the character counter directly to the message input enhances assistive tech feedback. Disabled select placeholders function predictably under validation, while successful ticket submissions correctly leverage dynamic alert regions for screen reader announcements.\n\nBootstrap dismiss buttons on high-contrast backgrounds require explicit dark outlines to meet visual contrast standards. Furthermore, narrow viewports risk horizontal overflow at small screen widths, requiring adjusted flex wrapping and scaled brand elements to maintain structural stability.\n\nRedundant utility classes in the mobile navigation collapse create uneven spacing, requiring clean margin normalization. Meanwhile, reducing hero header padding at narrow viewports optimizes above-the-fold content framing, while inspecting the store locator grid confirms responsive column transitions and proportional map canvas sizing for mobile viewports.\n\nNarrow viewport calculations for map pins confirm boundary safety, but shrinking the map label font size and max-width on mobile prevents edge clipping. FAQ triggers also require compressed padding and font scales on small screens to prevent text wrapping issues and icon crowding.\n\nInput padding adjustments prevent text collision with the clear button in search inputs. PowerShell regex validation might suffice, but browser binaries in standard installation paths could offer more robust parsing if needed.\n\n","tool_calls":[{"name":"update_steps","args":{"steps":[{"status":"done","title":"Refine CSS accessibility and responsiveness"},{"status":"done","title":"Harden JS fallbacks and keyboard support"},{"status":"done","title":"Unify footer and accessibility in HTML pages"},{"status":"in_progress","title":"Verify HTML, CSS, JS, and layout checks"}]}},{"name":"update_focus","args":{"focus":"Running automated checks across HTML, CSS, JS, and assets"}},{"name":"run_command","args":{"CommandLine":"$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'\n$pages = @('pages/store-locator.html', 'pages/faq.html', 'pages/contact-support.html')\n$js = Get-Content 'js/support.js' -Raw\n$css = Get-Content 'css/support.css' -Raw\n\n# 1. Console / debug leftovers\n$consoleHits = Select-String -Path @('js/support.js') + $pages -Pattern 'console\\.|debugger|\\balert\\('\nif ($consoleHits) { Write-Error \"Found console/debug calls: $consoleHits\"; exit 1 }\nWrite-Output 'CHECK_CONSOLE_CLEAN=PASS'\n\n# 2. Logo.png file & fallbacks\nif (!(Test-Path 'images/Logo.png')) { Write-Error 'Missing images/Logo.png'; exit 1 }\nforeach ($p in $pages) {\n  $raw = Get-Content $p -Raw\n  $logoCount = ([regex]::Matches($raw, '\\.\\./images/Logo\\.png')).Count\n  $fallbackCount = ([regex]::Matches($raw, 'ud-brand-fallback')).Count\n  $onerrorCount = ([regex]::Matches($raw, 'is-missing')).Count\n  if ($logoCount -ne 2 -or $fallbackCount -ne 2 -or $onerrorCount -ne 2) {\n    Write-Error \"$p logo/fallback mismatch: logo=$logoCount fallback=$fallbackCount onerror=$onerrorCount\"\n    exit 1\n  }\n}\nif ($js -notmatch 'initLogoFallbacks' -or $css -notmatch '\\.ud-brand-logo\\.is-missing \\+ \\.ud-brand-fallback') {\n  Write-Error 'Logo fallback hook missing in JS or CSS'\n  exit 1\n}\nWrite-Output 'CHECK_LOGO_FALLBACKS=PASS'\n\n# 3. Unified Footer across all 3 pages\n$footers = @()\nforeach ($p in $pages) {\n  $raw = Get-Content $p -Raw\n  $m = [regex]::Match($raw, '(?s)<footer class=\"ud-footer\">.*?</footer>')\n  if (!$m.Success) { Write-Error \"Missing footer in $p\"; exit 1 }\n  $footers += $m.Value\n}\nif ($footers[0] -ne $footers[1] -or $footers[1] -ne $footers[2]) {\n  Write-Error 'Footers are NOT identical across all 3 pages'\n  exit 1\n}\nif ($footers[0] -notmatch 'URBANDROPS &copy; 2026' -or $footers[0] -notmatch 'Customer Service' -or $footers[0] -notmatch 'AI Image Disclosure') {\n  Write-Error 'Footer missing copyright, Customer Service, or AI Image Disclosure'\n  exit 1\n}\nWrite-Output 'CHECK_UNIFIED_FOOTER=PASS'\n\n# 4. Accessibility IDs & ARIA targets\nforeach ($p in $pages) {\n  $raw = Get-Content $p -Raw\n  if ($raw -notmatch 'href=\"#mainContent\"' -or $raw -notmatch 'id=\"mainContent\"') {\n    Write-Error \"Missing skip link or mainContent in $p\"\n    exit 1\n  }\n  foreach ($attr in @('aria-controls', 'aria-labelledby', 'aria-describedby')) {\n    $matches = [regex]::Matches($raw, $attr + '=\"([^\"]+)\"')\n    foreach ($m in $matches) {\n      $ids = $m.Groups[1].Value -split '\\s+'\n      foreach ($id in $ids) {\n        if ($raw -notmatch ('id=\"' + [regex]::Escape($id) + '\"')) {\n          Write-Error \"Broken $attr reference '$id' in $p\"\n          exit 1\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\nWrite-Output 'CHECK_ACCESSIBILITY_IDS=PASS'\n\n# 5. CSS focus-visible & mobile media queries\nif ($css -notmatch ':focus-visible' -or $css -notmatch '@media \\(max-width: 991\\.98px\\)' -or $css -notmatch '@media \\(max-width: 575\\.98px\\)') {\n  Write-Error 'Missing focus-visible or responsive media queries in CSS'\n  exit 1\n}\nWrite-Output 'CHECK_CSS_A11Y_RESPONSIVE=PASS'\n\n# 6. Check Edge/Chrome availability for optional headless DOM check\nGet-ChildItem 'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Microsoft\\Edge\\Application\\msedge.exe', 'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object FullName\n","WaitMsBeforeAsync":8000,"toolSummary":"Verify HTML, CSS, JS, and footer consistency"}}]}

Code Cleanup, Zero Console Logs/Errors & `../images/Logo.png` Fallbacks\n- Confirmed zero `console.*`, `debugger`, or `alert()` calls across [js/support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js), [pages/store-locator.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/store-locator.html), [pages/faq.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/faq.html), and [pages/contact-support.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/contact-support.html).\n- Verified [images/Logo.png](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/images/Logo.png) is a valid `208x208` PNG and preserved the inline `onerror` + `.ud-brand-fallback` (`UD` monogram) markup in both the navbar and footer across all three pages.\n- Added `initLogoFallbacks()` and defensive DOM guards (such as safe status node construction in `openDirectionsModal`, Bootstrap modal fallback toast if CDN JS is unavailable, and null checks in `setFieldFeedback`) in [js/support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js).\n\n### 2. Unified Dark Footer Layout\n- Standardized the `<footer class=\"ud-footer\">` markup across [pages/store-locator.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/store-locator.html), [pages/faq.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/faq.html), and [pages/contact-support.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/contact-support.html) so all three share an identical layout containing:\n  - Brand logo (`../images/Logo.png` + fallback), Navigation links, and **Customer Service** links (`FAQ & Policies`, `Shipping Info`, `Returns & Exchanges`, `Sizing Guide`, `Contact Support`).\n  - **AI Image Disclosure** notice (`.ud-footer-disclosure` in [css/support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css)).\n  - Copyright line `URBANDROPS Â© 2026. ALL RIGHTS RESERVED.` alongside `ISB // LHR // KHI`.\n\n### 3. Keyboard Accessibility & Mobile Responsiveness\n- Added a `.ud-skip-link` (`Skip to main content` targeting `#mainContent`) and clear `:focus-visible` outlines for links, buttons, form controls, `.ud-faq-trigger`, `.ud-footer-link`, and `.ud-ticket-confirmation-card .btn-close` in [css/support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css).\n- Enhanced FAQ accordion semantics (`role=\"region\"`, `aria-labelledby`, `visibility: hidden` on collapsed panels, `aria-pressed` on category filter pills, and `ArrowUp`/`ArrowDown`/`Home`/`End` navigation in addition to `Tab` order) in [pages/faq.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/faq.html) and [js/support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js).\n- Added `aria-pressed` state syncing on store focus buttons and map pins in [js/support.js](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/js/support.js), `aria-describedby=\"messageFeedback messageCharCount\"` and focus restoration on confirmation card dismissal in [pages/contact-support.html](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/pages/contact-support.html).\n- Refined mobile styles (`@media (max-width: 991.98px)` and `@media (max-width: 575.98px)`) in [css/support.css](file:///c:/Users/laptop%20villa/SE-B_Group05_URBANDROP/css/support.css) for ticker wrapping, navbar brand sizing, map canvas/grid label fit, FAQ triggers, and full-width mobile toast placement.\n\n### Verification\n- Static PowerShell verification passed for all 5 checks: `CHECK_CONSOLE_CLEAN=PASS`, `CHECK_LOGO_FALLBACKS=PASS`, `CHECK_UNIFIED_FOOTER=PASS`
